## Prior Review Findings

- id: R-1
  source: blind-review.md; review-edge-case-hunter.md
  title: Canonical flow-id hydration leaves `responseSpec.relatedFlowId` stale
  detail: Restart normalization now canonicalizes persisted `responseSpec` payloads during whitespace-only flow-id matches, so a stored `relatedFlowId` such as `"flow-1 "` is rewritten before response reuse or reattachment.
  location: src/stately-studio/actors/shared/restart-hydration.ts:109-141; tests/stately-studio/restart-hydration.test.ts:214-237

- id: R-2
  source: blind-review.md; review-edge-case-hunter.md
  title: Malformed pending create-dialogue proposals can survive hydration and crash replay
  detail: Restart normalization now rejects malformed pending proposals missing required nested draft bundles, so null/undefined entries and partial proposal objects are skipped before replay handling can dereference them.
  location: src/stately-studio/actors/shared/restart-hydration.ts:126-178; tests/stately-studio/restart-hydration.test.ts:263-294

- Restart hydration only recreates one eligible create flow and can miss additional restartable flows or malformed hydration keys.
- Rehydration payloads drop persisted lifecycle phase, so the recreated child cannot distinguish `dialogue_collecting`, `review_ready`, or `save_pending`.
- Collision-safe hydration still leaves dependent flow references stale when canonical IDs change; `focusedFlowId`, `recentFlowIds`, and nested `responseBaseline.flowId` values must be rewritten alongside the active-flow key.
- Restart hydration loses the create child’s hydrated phase at the supervisor boundary unless the response supervisor carries the persisted phase/current machine state through root restart bookkeeping.
- Root defers child recreation until the next flow-first turn instead of recreating live create dialogue when active workflows are reloaded.
- Restart hydration drops the child supervisor patch unless the root patch includes `focusedFlowId` and `currentTurn.currentMachineStateValue` from the response supervisor output.
- Hydration ignores the normalized `currentMachineStateValue` fallback, causing migrated records without `snapshotRef.phase` to resume as `dialogue_collecting`.
- Phase-specific hydration can succeed with a blank `flowId`, allowing invalid identifiers to be persisted as supervised results.
- Hydrated flow-ID collisions can reopen the wrong record when alias resolution is last-write-wins instead of preserving all normalized targets.
- Collision renaming leaves nested `createPlan.flowId` and `pendingCreateDialogueProposals[].flowId` stale unless they are rewritten with the hydrated key.
- id: EC-1
  source: edge-case-review
  title: Hydrated flow renaming leaves `responseSpec.relatedFlowId` stale
  classification: patch
  detail: Restart normalization rewrites `createPlan.flowId`, `pendingCreateDialogueProposals[].flowId`, and `responseBaseline.flowId`, but it does not rewrite the persisted `responseSpec` payload. Because `prepareResponse()` carries `relatedFlowId` forward and `applyResponseDraftToWorkflowPromptState()` only reattaches drafts when `relatedFlowId` matches the record’s current `flowId`, a renamed hydrated flow can keep emitting drafts tagged with the pre-hydration id and fail to attach to the canonical record.
  location: src/stately-studio/chatsendrootmachinev2.ts:515-547; src/stately-studio/actors/shared/response/response-spec-builders.ts:86-127; src/stately-studio/actors/shared/workflow-registry.ts:1170-1175

- id: EC-2
  source: edge-case-review
  title: Malformed `pendingCreateDialogueProposals` entries can crash restart normalization
  classification: patch
  detail: Hydration only guards `record.pendingCreateDialogueProposals` with `Array.isArray(...)` before dereferencing `proposal.flowId` and spreading each entry. If a persisted record contains a null, undefined, or otherwise malformed proposal item, restart hydration can throw instead of skipping the bad entry or failing cleanly.
  location: src/stately-studio/chatsendrootmachinev2.ts:537-545
## Dev Notes

### Architecture alignment

- The approved architecture explicitly says that on restart, root reloads active workflow records and, for any non-terminal create flow, recreates `createDialogueMachine(flowId)` from persisted workflow state. [Source: `_bmad-output/planning-artifacts/architecture.md` — Shutdown and restart behavior]
- The architecture also says child-local state is derived/disposable and root/workflow record owns durable truth. Rehydration must use persisted workflow artifacts, not child-local snapshots as the sole source of truth. [Source: `_bmad-output/planning-artifacts/architecture.md` — Durable vs transient ownership]
- The create-dialogue child’s start/resume input is expected to include workflow artifacts plus `campaignEntityIndex` and `draftRevision`. [Source: `_bmad-output/planning-artifacts/architecture.md` — Entity context and lookup support]
- Root is the durable orchestration and policy boundary and owns restart/resume coordination. [Source: `_bmad-output/planning-artifacts/architecture.md` — Responsibility boundaries for `chatsendrootmachinev2`]

### Previous story intelligence

- Story 2.1 established the response supervisor vs render split and the existence of a flow-keyed create-dialogue child surface; Story 4.2 should restore that child on restart rather than rebuilding dialogue continuity somewhere else. [Source: `_bmad-output/implementation-artifacts/2-1-split-response-rendering-from-response-supervision.md`]
- Story 2.2 established flow-first ingress into the active create-dialogue child, which makes restart rehydration necessary for conversational continuity after process loss. [Source: `_bmad-output/implementation-artifacts/2-2-route-active-create-turns-through-flow-first-ingress.md`]
- Story 2.3 established that the child should receive read-only `campaignEntityIndex`; Story 4.2 should restore that input from root on restart as part of the same rehydration contract. [Source: `_bmad-output/implementation-artifacts/2-3-provide-campaign-entity-index-to-the-create-dialogue-child.md`]
- Stories 1.1–1.3 established the durable workflow record, stable `flowId`, and baseline/revision surfaces that restart rehydration depends on. [Source: Epic 1 story artifacts]
- Story 4.1 established distinct phases like `review_ready` and `save_pending`; Story 4.2 should restore the correct child behavior under the persisted phase rather than guessing from response text. [Source: `_bmad-output/implementation-artifacts/4-1-separate-review-ready-and-save-pending-phases-under-one-flow.md`]

### Current code reality to build on

- Root already maintains `activeFlows`, `focusedFlowId`, and `recentFlowIds`, making the workflow registry the natural restart source for reconstructing active create flows. [Source: `src/stately-studio/chatsendrootmachinev2.ts`; `src/stately-studio/actors/shared/workflow-registry.ts`]
- `resolveActiveWorkflowMatch()` already exists as a workflow-registry helper, which may be useful for active-flow selection, though restart logic will likely need explicit iteration over non-terminal create records rather than only the currently focused flow. [Source: `src/stately-studio/actors/shared/workflow-registry.ts`]
- `responsemachine.ts` still shows a limited top-level input surface in the brownfield code (`responseSpec` only in the currently inspected shape), which is a sign that restart-ready create-dialogue supervisor input assembly remains to be completed. [Source: `src/stately-studio/responsemachine.ts` search results]
- Shared machine protocol helpers already include workflow lifecycle cleanup/shutdown helpers like `clearWorkflowLifecycleOnMatchingComplete`, which is adjacent to but distinct from the restart-time recreation problem. Story 4.2 should focus on recreation, not shutdown. [Source: `src/stately-studio/actors/shared/protocols/machine-setup.ts`]
- `createmachine.ts` still manages `activeFlowId` / `flowStartedAt` / `timeoutAt` and workflow-bound behavior, but restart architecture says create can be recreated lazily when adjudication or persistence is needed. [Source: `src/stately-studio/createmachine.ts`; `_bmad-output/planning-artifacts/architecture.md`]

### Implementation guidance for the dev agent

- Rehydrate from persisted root-owned workflow artifacts, not from response child memory.
- Keep the same `flowId` through restart and child recreation.
- Restore only what is needed for dialogue continuity; avoid eagerly reconstructing more live actors than the architecture requires.
- Use persisted phase metadata to determine how the recreated child should resume.
- If a shared helper is needed to build create-dialogue resume input from a `WorkflowRecord`, place it near workflow/response orchestration code rather than scattering conversion logic inline.

### Suggested file touch list

- `src/stately-studio/chatsendrootmachinev2.ts`
- `src/stately-studio/responsemachine.ts`
- the create-dialogue child machine file introduced in Epic 2
- `src/stately-studio/actors/shared/workflow-registry.ts`
- `src/stately-studio/actors/workflow-reducer.ts` only if typed persisted workflow surfaces need small refinements for restart

### Project Structure Notes

- Keep restart/rehydration work inside `src/stately-studio/` root/response/workflow orchestration modules.
- Do not move restart continuity into UI/session code or generic server modules.
- Preserve the architecture boundary: root reloads and rehydrates, response supervises the recreated child, create remains lazy for adjudication/persistence.

### Testing Requirements

- Add the following tests:
  - one assertion that non-terminal create workflow records can recreate the flow-keyed create-dialogue child on restart
  - one assertion that workflow truth survives loss of child-local memory because root-owned artifacts drive rehydration
  - one assertion that the same `flowId` and persisted phase are preserved after recreation
  - avoid shutdown-specific tests until Story 4.3 lands
- Prioritize restart-path verification and architecture alignment over suite expansion.

### XState / Stately alignment

- Parent/root should own restart-time actor recreation from persisted state.
- Child actors should be reconstructible from explicit typed input.
- Child-local memory should remain disposable.
- State continuity should come from parent-owned persisted context and contracts, not implicit live runtime memory.

### Completion Notes List

- Story target confirmed from approved architecture + epics: Epic 4, Story 4.2.
- Root now accepts optional `workflowHydration` state so persisted active workflows can be restored into the root registry on restart.
- Added restart-safe create-dialogue resume input assembly from root-owned workflow artifacts, including persisted drafts, response baseline, revision metadata, feedback, and read-only campaign entity context.
- Rehydration now treats child-local dialogue memory as disposable and keeps response/create ownership boundaries intact.
- Restart hydration now canonicalizes hydrated active-flow identifiers and returns every eligible restartable create flow instead of collapsing to one candidate.
- Persisted create lifecycle phase now round-trips through restart resume input, response supervision, and create-dialogue rehydration.
- Root startup now eagerly replays restartable create workflows through the response supervisor before normal turn ingress resumes.
- Restart hydration now skips terminal create records, preserves restart results in root-owned hydration bookkeeping, and tracks per-flow rehydrated phases.
- `create-dialogue-machine.ts` now consumes the persisted lifecycle phase to route through phase-specific hydration states before reporting supervision.
- Root restart hydration now canonicalizes dependent flow references, rewrites nested response baselines to the hydrated flow ID, and carries the create phase/current-machine state through the response supervisor boundary.
- Restart hydration now merges the supervised child patch back into root hydration results, falls back to normalized `currentMachineStateValue`, rejects blank phase-specific flow IDs, preserves duplicate-source aliases collision-safely, and rewrites nested create-plan/proposal flow IDs when hydrated keys are renamed.
- Restart hydration now rewrites persisted `responseSpec` payloads to the hydrated flow ID and skips malformed pending proposal entries during canonicalization.
- Restart hydration now canonicalizes whitespace-padded `responseSpec.relatedFlowId` values and rejects malformed pending proposals missing required nested draft bundles.
- Restart hydration now preserves valid pending proposals while skipping malformed partial bundles, and root/live proposal handling now rejects partial bundles before authority evaluation.
- Rejected proposal handling now normalizes feedback to an array before merge, preventing non-iterable payloads from crashing the reducer path.
- Restart hydration now rewrites top-level and baseline `responseSpec.relatedFlowId` values when canonicalized flow IDs differ.
- Added regression coverage in `tests/stately-studio/restart-hydration.test.ts` and `tests/stately-studio/workflow-reducer.test.ts` for malformed proposals, non-array feedback, and responseSpec reattachment.

# Story 4.2: Rehydrate create dialogue from persisted workflow state on restart

Status: ready-for-dev

<!-- Note: Validation is optional. Run validate-create-story for quality check before dev-story. -->

## Story

As a platform engineer,
I want active create dialogue actors to be reconstructible from workflow state,
so that workflow continuity survives process restarts or actor loss.

## Acceptance Criteria

1. Given the process restarts during an active create flow, when root reloads active workflow records, then it can recreate the `createDialogueMachine(flowId)` child from persisted workflow artifacts.
2. Given child-local memory is lost, when the child is recreated, then workflow truth remains intact because root owned the authoritative state.

## Sequencing / Dependencies

- Target confirmed: Epic 4, Story 4.2.
- Required predecessors: Epic 1 stories 1.1–1.3, Epic 2 stories 2.1–2.3, Epic 3 stories 3.1–3.3, and Story 4.1, because restart rehydration depends on durable workflow state, stable `flowId`, baseline/versioning, supervised dialogue routing, lookup and adjudication contracts, plus distinct lifecycle phase modeling.
- This story is a prerequisite for Story 4.3 terminal live-actor shutdown coordination.
- If repo behavior conflicts with older assumptions, prefer `_bmad-output/planning-artifacts/architecture.md`.

## Tasks / Subtasks

- [x] Add restart-ready rehydration input assembly in root for active create workflows (AC: 1)
  - [x] On root startup/reload, inspect persisted active workflow records and identify non-terminal create flows that should have a live create-dialogue child.
  - [x] Reconstruct create-dialogue start/resume input from persisted workflow artifacts rather than child-local memory.
  - [x] Preserve the same `flowId` and lifecycle phase when rebuilding the child.

- [x] Recreate the flow-keyed `createDialogueMachine(flowId)` child from persisted workflow state (AC: 1)
  - [x] Use the response supervisor path introduced in Epic 2 to recreate the create-dialogue child for each eligible active flow.
  - [x] Provide the child with persisted workflow artifacts needed for continuity, including `createPlan`, authoritative `createDrafts`, `responseSpec`, feedback, revision/baseline metadata, and read-only `campaignEntityIndex` where applicable.
  - [x] Keep the recreated child keyed to the same `flowId` used by root and create.

- [x] Treat child-local memory as disposable and reconstructible (AC: 2)
  - [x] Do not rely on transient prompt assembly state, scratch retrieval state, or other child-local memory as the only source of workflow truth.
  - [x] Ensure the rehydrated child can continue from root-owned persisted artifacts even if all local child memory was lost.
  - [x] Document clearly in code/comments which child-local fields are disposable versus which artifacts must come from root persistence.

  responseSpec: AnyResponseSpec | null;
  sentAt: number;
}): WorkflowResponseBaseline {
  return {
    flowId: input.flowId,
    draftRevision: input.draftRevision,
    createDrafts:
      cloneEntityCreationDraftBundle(input.createDrafts) ?? input.createDrafts,
    responseSpec: cloneResponseSpec(input.responseSpec) ?? null,
    sentAt: input.sentAt,
  };
}

export function resolveCreateWorkflowDraftRevision(
  record: WorkflowRecord,
): number | null {
  if (
    typeof record.responseBaseline?.draftRevision === "number" &&
    Number.isFinite(record.responseBaseline.draftRevision)
  ) {
    return record.responseBaseline.draftRevision;
  }

  if (
    typeof record.snapshotRef?.responseBaselineRevision === "number" &&
    Number.isFinite(record.snapshotRef.responseBaselineRevision)
  ) {
    return record.snapshotRef.responseBaselineRevision;
  }

  if (
    typeof record.snapshotRef?.draftRevision === "number" &&
    Number.isFinite(record.snapshotRef.draftRevision)
  ) {
    return record.snapshotRef.draftRevision;
  }

  if (hasRestartableCreateWorkflowArtifacts(record)) {
    return 0;
  }

  return null;

function buildEntityCreationDraftBundle(
  createPlan: CreatePlan,
  prefilledCreateFieldValues: Record<string, unknown>,
): EntityCreationDraftBundle {
  return {
    coreDraft: buildCoreDraft(createPlan),
    fieldDrafts: buildFieldDraftCollection(
      createPlan.fields,
      prefilledCreateFieldValues,
    ),
    statBlockDrafts: buildStatBlockDrafts(createPlan.statBlocks),
    progressionDraft: buildProgressionDraft(createPlan.progression),
    relationDrafts: buildRelationDraftCollection(createPlan.relations),
  };
}

export function resolveEntityCreationDrafts(
  input: ResolveEntityCreationDraftsInput,
): ResolveEntityCreationDraftsOutput {
  const drafts = buildEntityCreationDraftBundle(
    input.createPlan,
    input.prefilledCreateFieldValues,
  );

  return patchReady({
    createDrafts: drafts,
    planningBlockers: [],
  });
}

function normalizeCreateDialogueLookupRequestPayload(input: {
  context: ChatSendRootMachineV2Context;
  payload: CreateDialogueLookupRequestPayload;
}): CreateDialogueLookupRequestPayload {
  const resolved = resolveCreateDialogueLookupRequestEntityFromIndex({
    campaignEntityIndex: input.context.campaignEntityIndex,
    request: input.payload,
  });

  return {
    ...input.payload,
    lookup: {
      ...input.payload.lookup,
      entityId: resolved.entityId ?? input.payload.lookup.entityId ?? null,
      entityLabel: resolved.entityLabel ?? input.payload.lookup.entityLabel ?? null,
    },
  };
}

function buildCreateMachineAdjudicateProposalEvent(input: {
  context: ChatSendRootMachineV2Context;
  event: RootCreateDialogueProposalSubmittedEvent;
}): CreateMachineAdjudicateProposalEvent | null {
  if (!isValidCreateDialogueProposalSubmittedPayload(input.event.payload)) {
    return null;
  }

  const normalizedFlowId = input.event.payload.flowId.trim();
  const normalizedPayload = {
    ...input.event.payload,
    flowId: normalizedFlowId,
  };
  const activeFlow = input.context.activeFlows[normalizedFlowId] ?? null;
  if (!activeFlow) return null;
  if (isStaleCreateDialogueProposal({ record: activeFlow, proposal: normalizedPayload })) {
    return null;
  }

  const createPlan = activeFlow.createPlan ?? null;
  const currentDrafts = activeFlow.createDrafts ?? activeFlow.responseBaseline?.createDrafts ?? null;
  if (!createPlan || !currentDrafts) return null;

  const authorityDecision = evaluateCreateDraftMutationAuthority({
    flowId: normalizedFlowId,
    createPlan,
    currentDrafts,
    proposedDrafts: normalizedPayload.proposedDrafts,
    source: normalizedPayload.source,
  });

import {
  buildWorkflowRecordFromStartEvent,
  areWorkflowValuesEqual,
  patchWorkflowRecord,
  pushRecentFlowId,
  isStaleCreateDialogueProposal,
  buildStaleCreateDialogueProposalFeedback,
  resolveCreateWorkflowDraftRevision,
  isWellFormedCreateWorkflowStartEventPayload,
  type WorkflowStatus,
  type WorkflowAwaitingReviewEventPayload,
  type WorkflowBlockedEventPayload,
  type WorkflowCompleteEventPayload,
  type CreateDialogueProposalSubmittedPayload,
  type WorkflowFeedbackItem,
  type WorkflowNeedConfirmationEventPayload,
  type WorkflowNeedInfoEventPayload,
  type WorkflowRecord,
  type WorkflowStartEventPayload,
  type WorkflowStartProjection,
  type WorkflowTimedOutEventPayload,
  type WorkflowUpdateEventPayload,
} from "./shared/workflow-registry";
import { evaluateCreateDraftMutationAuthority } from "./shared/create-draft-mutation-policy";
import type {
  ChatSendRootMachineV2Context,
  RootWorkflowAwaitingReviewEvent,
  RootWorkflowBlockedEvent,
  RootWorkflowCompleteEvent,
  RootWorkflowNeedConfirmationEvent,
  RootWorkflowNeedInfoEvent,
  RootWorkflowStartEvent,
  RootWorkflowTimedOutEvent,
  RootWorkflowUpdateEvent,
} from "../chatsendrootmachinev2";

const TERMINAL_WORKFLOW_STATUSES = new Set<WorkflowStatus>([
  "blocked",
  "timed_out",
  "completed",
  "cancelled",
  "errored",
]);

function resolveOptionalIntentFlag(
  intent: "found" | "not_found" | null,
): boolean | undefined {
  if (intent === "found") return true;
  if (intent === "not_found") return false;

function isNonArrayObject(value: unknown): value is Record<string, unknown> {
  return typeof value === "object" && value !== null && !Array.isArray(value);
}

function hasRequiredProposedDraftGroups(value: unknown): boolean {
  if (!isNonArrayObject(value)) {
    return false;
  }

  return (
    isNonArrayObject(value.coreDraft) &&
    isNonArrayObject(value.fieldDrafts) &&
    Array.isArray(value.statBlockDrafts) &&
    isNonArrayObject(value.progressionDraft) &&
    isNonArrayObject(value.relationDrafts)
  );
}

export function isValidCreateDialogueProposalSubmittedPayload(
  payload: CreateDialogueProposalSubmittedPayload | null | undefined,
): payload is CreateDialogueProposalSubmittedPayload {
  if (!isNonArrayObject(payload)) {
    return false;
  }

  if (typeof payload.flowId !== "string" || payload.flowId.trim().length === 0) {
    return false;
  }

  if (typeof payload.baseDraftRevision !== "number" || !Number.isFinite(payload.baseDraftRevision)) {
    return false;
  }

  return hasRequiredProposedDraftGroups(payload.proposedDrafts);
}
  return undefined;
}

function isStaleOrDuplicateWorkflowEvent(
  record: WorkflowRecord,
  payload: {
    lastRequestEventId?: string;
    lastEventSequence?: number;
  },
): boolean {
  if (
    typeof payload.lastRequestEventId === "string" &&
    payload.lastRequestEventId.length > 0 &&
    payload.lastRequestEventId === record.lastRequestEventId
  ) {
    return true;
  }

  if (
    typeof payload.lastEventSequence === "number" &&
    Number.isFinite(payload.lastEventSequence) &&
    typeof record.lastEventSequence === "number" &&
    Number.isFinite(record.lastEventSequence) &&
    payload.lastEventSequence <= record.lastEventSequence
  ) {
    return true;
  }

  return false;
}

function buildTerminalWorkflowCleanup(context: ChatSendRootMachineV2Context, flowId: string) {
  const nextActiveFlows = { ...context.activeFlows };
  delete nextActiveFlows[flowId];

  return {
    activeFlows: nextActiveFlows,
    focusedFlowId:
      context.focusedFlowId === flowId ? null : context.focusedFlowId,
    recentFlowIds: pushRecentFlowId(context.recentFlowIds, flowId),
  };
}

function cloneWorkflowFeedbackItems(
  value: WorkflowFeedbackItem[] | undefined,
): WorkflowFeedbackItem[] {
  return Array.isArray(value) ? value.map((item) => ({ ...item })) : [];
}

function dedupeWorkflowFeedbackItems(
  ...groups: Array<WorkflowFeedbackItem[] | undefined>
): WorkflowFeedbackItem[] {
  const deduped: WorkflowFeedbackItem[] = [];

  for (const group of groups) {
    for (const item of cloneWorkflowFeedbackItems(group)) {
      const isDuplicate = deduped.some(
        (existingItem) =>
          existingItem.target === item.target &&
          existingItem.message === item.message &&
          existingItem.code === item.code,
      );

      if (!isDuplicate) {
        deduped.push(item);
      }
    }
  }

  return deduped;
}

function normalizeCreateDialogueProposalForComparison(
  proposal: CreateDialogueProposalSubmittedPayload,
): CreateDialogueProposalSubmittedPayload {
  return {
    ...proposal,
    feedback: cloneWorkflowFeedbackItems(proposal.feedback),
  };
}

function isDuplicateCreateDialogueProposal(
  left: CreateDialogueProposalSubmittedPayload,
  right: CreateDialogueProposalSubmittedPayload,
): boolean {
  return areWorkflowValuesEqual(
    normalizeCreateDialogueProposalForComparison(left),
    normalizeCreateDialogueProposalForComparison(right),
  );
}

export function buildWorkflowStartProjection(
  turn: ChatSendRootMachineV2Context["currentTurn"],
): WorkflowStartProjection {
  return {
    routeTarget: turn.routeTarget ?? undefined,
    entityId: turn.entityId ?? undefined,
    title: turn.title ?? undefined,
    entityIdCandidates: turn.entityIdCandidates ?? undefined,
    titleCandidates: turn.titleCandidates ?? undefined,
    mentionedEntityIds:
      turn.mentionedEntityIds.length > 0 ? turn.mentionedEntityIds : undefined,
    fieldHints: turn.fieldHints ?? undefined,
    collectedFieldValues:
      Object.keys(turn.contentCreateFieldValues).length > 0
        ? turn.contentCreateFieldValues
        : undefined,
    lastRequestEventId: turn.eventId ?? undefined,
    lastEventSequence: turn.lastEventSequence ?? undefined,
    deicticResolution: turn.deicticResolution ?? undefined,
    mutationIntent: resolveOptionalIntentFlag(turn.mutationIntent),
    lookupIntent: resolveOptionalIntentFlag(turn.lookupIntent),
    currentMachineStateValue: turn.currentMachineStateValue ?? undefined,
  };
}

export function reduceWorkflowStart(
  context: ChatSendRootMachineV2Context,
  payload: WorkflowStartEventPayload,
): Pick<
  ChatSendRootMachineV2Context,
  "activeFlows" | "focusedFlowId" | "recentFlowIds"
> {
  const now = Date.now();
  const projection = buildWorkflowStartProjection(context.currentTurn);

  if (!isWellFormedCreateWorkflowStartEventPayload(payload)) {
    return {
      activeFlows: context.activeFlows,
      focusedFlowId: context.focusedFlowId,
      recentFlowIds: context.recentFlowIds,
    };
  }

  const existing = context.activeFlows[payload.flowId];
  if (existing && isStaleOrDuplicateWorkflowEvent(existing, payload)) {
    return {
      activeFlows: context.activeFlows,
      focusedFlowId: context.focusedFlowId,
      recentFlowIds: context.recentFlowIds,
    };
  }
  const startedRecord = buildWorkflowRecordFromStartEvent({
    event: payload,
    projection,
    now,
  });

  const nextRecord = existing
    ? {
        ...existing,
        ...startedRecord,
        timing: {
          ...existing.timing,
          ...startedRecord.timing,
          createdAt: existing.timing.createdAt,
          updatedAt: now,
        },
      }
    : startedRecord;

  return {
    activeFlows: {
      ...context.activeFlows,
      [payload.flowId]: nextRecord,
    },
    focusedFlowId: payload.flowId,
    recentFlowIds: pushRecentFlowId(context.recentFlowIds, payload.flowId),
  };
}

export function reduceWorkflowUpdate(
  context: ChatSendRootMachineV2Context,
  payload: WorkflowUpdateEventPayload,
): Pick<
  ChatSendRootMachineV2Context,
  "activeFlows" | "focusedFlowId" | "recentFlowIds"
> {
  const existing = context.activeFlows[payload.flowId];
  if (!existing) {
    return {
      activeFlows: context.activeFlows,
      focusedFlowId: context.focusedFlowId,
      recentFlowIds: context.recentFlowIds,
    };
  }
  if (isStaleOrDuplicateWorkflowEvent(existing, payload)) {
    return {
      activeFlows: context.activeFlows,
      focusedFlowId: context.focusedFlowId,
      recentFlowIds: context.recentFlowIds,
    };
  }

  const now = Date.now();
  const updated = patchWorkflowRecord({
    record: existing,
    event: payload,
    now,
  });

  if (payload.status && TERMINAL_WORKFLOW_STATUSES.has(payload.status)) {
    return buildTerminalWorkflowCleanup(context, payload.flowId);
  }

  return {
    activeFlows: {
      ...context.activeFlows,
      [payload.flowId]: updated,
    },
    focusedFlowId:
      payload.status === "waiting_for_user" ||
      payload.status === "need_info" ||
      payload.status === "need_confirmation" ||
      payload.status === "awaiting_review"
        ? payload.flowId
        : context.focusedFlowId,
    recentFlowIds: context.recentFlowIds,
  };
}

export function reduceCreateDialogueProposalSubmitted(
  context: ChatSendRootMachineV2Context,
  payload: CreateDialogueProposalSubmittedPayload,
): Pick<
  ChatSendRootMachineV2Context,
  "activeFlows" | "focusedFlowId" | "recentFlowIds"
> {
  if (!isValidCreateDialogueProposalSubmittedPayload(payload)) {
    return {
      activeFlows: context.activeFlows,
      focusedFlowId: context.focusedFlowId,
      recentFlowIds: context.recentFlowIds,
    };
  }

  const normalizedPayload = {
    ...payload,
    flowId: payload.flowId.trim(),
  };
  const existing = context.activeFlows[normalizedPayload.flowId];
  if (!existing) {
    return {
      activeFlows: context.activeFlows,
      focusedFlowId: context.focusedFlowId,
      recentFlowIds: context.recentFlowIds,
    };
  }

  if (isStaleCreateDialogueProposal({ record: existing, proposal: normalizedPayload })) {
    const expectedDraftRevision = resolveCreateWorkflowDraftRevision(existing) ?? 0;
    const staleFeedback = buildStaleCreateDialogueProposalFeedback({
      flowId: normalizedPayload.flowId,
      expectedDraftRevision,
      receivedDraftRevision: normalizedPayload.baseDraftRevision,
    });

    const nextFeedback = dedupeWorkflowFeedbackItems(
      existing.feedback,
      staleFeedback,
      normalizedPayload.feedback,
    );

    return {
      activeFlows: {
        ...context.activeFlows,
        [payload.flowId]: {
          ...existing,
          feedback: nextFeedback,
        },
      },
      focusedFlowId: context.focusedFlowId,
      recentFlowIds: context.recentFlowIds,
    };
  }

  const createPlan = existing.createPlan ?? null;
  const currentDrafts = existing.createDrafts ?? existing.responseBaseline?.createDrafts ?? null;
  if (createPlan && currentDrafts) {
    const authorityDecision = evaluateCreateDraftMutationAuthority({
      flowId: normalizedPayload.flowId,
      createPlan,
      currentDrafts,
      proposedDrafts: normalizedPayload.proposedDrafts,
    });

    if (!authorityDecision.allowed) {
      const submittedFeedback = Array.isArray(normalizedPayload.feedback)
        ? normalizedPayload.feedback
        : [];
      const mergedFeedback = [
        ...(existing.feedback ?? []),
        ...submittedFeedback,
        ...authorityDecision.feedback,
      ];

      const nextRecord: WorkflowRecord = {
        ...existing,
        feedback: mergedFeedback,
      };

      return {
        activeFlows: {
          ...context.activeFlows,
          [payload.flowId]: nextRecord,
        },
        focusedFlowId: context.focusedFlowId,
        recentFlowIds: context.recentFlowIds,
      };
    }
  }

  const pendingCreateDialogueProposals =
    existing.pendingCreateDialogueProposals ?? [];
  const hasDuplicatePendingCreateDialogueProposal =
    pendingCreateDialogueProposals.some((proposal) =>
      isDuplicateCreateDialogueProposal(proposal, normalizedPayload),
    );

  if (hasDuplicatePendingCreateDialogueProposal) {
    return {
      activeFlows: context.activeFlows,
      focusedFlowId: context.focusedFlowId,
      recentFlowIds: context.recentFlowIds,
    };
  }

  const nextRecord: WorkflowRecord = {
    ...existing,
    pendingCreateDialogueProposals: [
      ...pendingCreateDialogueProposals,
      normalizedPayload,
    ],
  };

  return {
    activeFlows: {
      ...context.activeFlows,
      [payload.flowId]: nextRecord,
    },
    focusedFlowId: context.focusedFlowId,
    recentFlowIds: context.recentFlowIds,
  };
}

export function reduceWorkflowComplete(
  context: ChatSendRootMachineV2Context,
  payload: WorkflowCompleteEventPayload,
): Pick<
  ChatSendRootMachineV2Context,
  "activeFlows" | "focusedFlowId" | "recentFlowIds"
> {
  const existing = context.activeFlows[payload.flowId];
  if (!existing) {
    return {
      activeFlows: context.activeFlows,
      focusedFlowId: context.focusedFlowId,
      recentFlowIds: context.recentFlowIds,
    };
  }
  if (isStaleOrDuplicateWorkflowEvent(existing, payload)) {
    return {
      activeFlows: context.activeFlows,
      focusedFlowId: context.focusedFlowId,
      recentFlowIds: context.recentFlowIds,
    };
  }

  const now = Date.now();
  const terminalRecord: WorkflowRecord = {
    ...existing,
    status: payload.disposition,
    summary: payload.summary ?? existing.summary,
    snapshotRef: payload.snapshotRef ?? existing.snapshotRef,
    timing: {
      ...existing.timing,
      updatedAt: now,
    },
  };

  return buildTerminalWorkflowCleanup(context, terminalRecord.flowId);
}

export function toWorkflowStartPayloadFromRootEvent(
  event: RootWorkflowStartEvent,
): WorkflowStartEventPayload {
  return event.payload;
}

export function toWorkflowUpdatePayloadFromRootEvent(
  event: RootWorkflowUpdateEvent,
): WorkflowUpdateEventPayload {
  return event.payload;
}

export function toWorkflowCompletePayloadFromRootEvent(
  event: RootWorkflowCompleteEvent,
): WorkflowCompleteEventPayload {
  return event.payload;
}

export function toWorkflowUpdatePayloadFromWorkflowNeedInfoEvent(
  event: RootWorkflowNeedInfoEvent,
): WorkflowUpdateEventPayload {
  const payload = event.payload as WorkflowNeedInfoEventPayload;
  return {
    flowId: payload.flowId,
    status: "need_info",
    responseSpec: payload.responseSpec,
    missingFields: [payload.missingFieldKey],
    fieldHints: [payload.missingFieldKey],
    lastRequestEventId: payload.lastRequestEventId,
    lastEventSequence: payload.lastEventSequence,
  };
}

export function toWorkflowUpdatePayloadFromWorkflowNeedConfirmationEvent(
  event: RootWorkflowNeedConfirmationEvent,
): WorkflowUpdateEventPayload {
  const payload = event.payload as WorkflowNeedConfirmationEventPayload;
  return {
    flowId: payload.flowId,
    status: "need_confirmation",
    responseSpec: payload.responseSpec,
    lastRequestEventId: payload.lastRequestEventId,
    lastEventSequence: payload.lastEventSequence,
  };
}

export function toWorkflowUpdatePayloadFromWorkflowAwaitingReviewEvent(
  event: RootWorkflowAwaitingReviewEvent,
): WorkflowUpdateEventPayload {
  const payload = event.payload as WorkflowAwaitingReviewEventPayload;
  return {
    flowId: payload.flowId,
    status: "awaiting_review",
    responseSpec: payload.responseSpec,
    summary: "create flow awaiting review confirmation",
    lastRequestEventId: payload.lastRequestEventId,
    lastEventSequence: payload.lastEventSequence,
  };
}

export function toWorkflowUpdatePayloadFromWorkflowBlockedEvent(
  event: RootWorkflowBlockedEvent,
): WorkflowUpdateEventPayload {
  const payload = event.payload as WorkflowBlockedEventPayload;
  return {
    flowId: payload.flowId,
    status: "blocked",
    summary: payload.reasons.join(", "),
    lastRequestEventId: payload.lastRequestEventId,
    lastEventSequence: payload.lastEventSequence,
  };
}

export function toWorkflowUpdatePayloadFromWorkflowTimedOutEvent(
  event: RootWorkflowTimedOutEvent,
): WorkflowUpdateEventPayload {
  const payload = event.payload as WorkflowTimedOutEventPayload;
  return {
    flowId: payload.flowId,
    status: "timed_out",
    summary: "create flow timed out",
    lastRequestEventId: payload.lastRequestEventId,
    lastEventSequence: payload.lastEventSequence,
  };
}

export function resolveWorkflowStart(input: {
  context: ChatSendRootMachineV2Context;
  event: RootWorkflowStartEvent;
}) {
  const payload = toWorkflowStartPayloadFromRootEvent(input.event);
  return reduceWorkflowStart(input.context, payload);
}

export function resolveWorkflowUpdate(input: {
  context: ChatSendRootMachineV2Context;
  event: RootWorkflowUpdateEvent;
}) {
  const payload = toWorkflowUpdatePayloadFromRootEvent(input.event);
  return reduceWorkflowUpdate(input.context, payload);
}

export function resolveWorkflowComplete(input: {
  context: ChatSendRootMachineV2Context;
  event: RootWorkflowCompleteEvent;
}) {
  const payload = toWorkflowCompletePayloadFromRootEvent(input.event);
  return reduceWorkflowComplete(input.context, payload);
}

export function resolveWorkflowNeedInfo(input: {
  context: ChatSendRootMachineV2Context;
  event: RootWorkflowNeedInfoEvent;
}) {
  const payload = toWorkflowUpdatePayloadFromWorkflowNeedInfoEvent(input.event);
  return reduceWorkflowUpdate(input.context, payload);
}

export function resolveWorkflowNeedConfirmation(input: {
  context: ChatSendRootMachineV2Context;
  event: RootWorkflowNeedConfirmationEvent;
}) {
  const payload = toWorkflowUpdatePayloadFromWorkflowNeedConfirmationEvent(
    input.event,
  );
  return reduceWorkflowUpdate(input.context, payload);
}

export function resolveWorkflowAwaitingReview(input: {
  context: ChatSendRootMachineV2Context;
  event: RootWorkflowAwaitingReviewEvent;
}) {
  const payload = toWorkflowUpdatePayloadFromWorkflowAwaitingReviewEvent(
    input.event,
  );
  return reduceWorkflowUpdate(input.context, payload);
}

export function resolveWorkflowBlocked(input: {
  context: ChatSendRootMachineV2Context;
  event: RootWorkflowBlockedEvent;
}) {
  const payload = toWorkflowUpdatePayloadFromWorkflowBlockedEvent(input.event);
  return reduceWorkflowUpdate(input.context, payload);
}

export function resolveWorkflowTimedOut(input: {
  context: ChatSendRootMachineV2Context;
  event: RootWorkflowTimedOutEvent;
}) {
  const payload = toWorkflowUpdatePayloadFromWorkflowTimedOutEvent(input.event);
  return reduceWorkflowUpdate(input.context, payload);
}

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

import {
  buildCreateReviewResponseSpec,
  buildCreateSuccessResponseSpec,
  buildWriteAttachedEntityRecordsActorInput,
  buildWriteCoreEntityRecordActorInput,
  resolveCreateFlowTimeoutDelayMs,
} from "../../src/stately-studio/create-machine-input-builders";
import type { CreateMachineContextState } from "../../src/stately-studio/createmachine";
import { resolveEntityCreationDrafts } from "../../src/stately-studio/actors/resolve-entity-creation-drafts";
import type { CreatePlan } from "../../src/stately-studio/actors/resolve-create-plan";
import { CANON_STATES, ENTITY_TYPES } from "../../src/domain/entities/core/types";
import {
  ENTITY_FIELD_TYPES,
  FIELD_VALUE_KINDS,
} from "../../src/domain/entities/fields/types";

function buildCreatePlan(): CreatePlan {
  return {
    flowId: "flow-1",
    campaignId: "campaign-1",
    userId: "user-1",
    campaignMemberId: "member-1",
    role: "OWNER" as unknown as CreateMachineContextState["role"],
    gameSystemId: null,
    core: {
      entityType: ENTITY_TYPES.NPC,
      title: "",
      titleLower: "",
      canonState: CANON_STATES.CANON,
    },
    fields: [
      {
        fieldType: ENTITY_FIELD_TYPES.DESCRIPTION,
        valueKind: FIELD_VALUE_KINDS.TEXT,
        orderIndex: 0,
        required: true,
      },
    ],
    statBlocks: [],
    progression: {
      required: false,
      columns: [],
    },
    relations: [],
  };
}

function buildContext(
  overrides: Partial<CreateMachineContextState> = {},
): CreateMachineContextState {
  const createPlan = overrides.createPlan ?? buildCreatePlan();
  const createDrafts =
    overrides.createDrafts ??
    resolveEntityCreationDrafts({
      createPlan,
      prefilledCreateFieldValues: {
        [ENTITY_FIELD_TYPES.DESCRIPTION]: "A sneaky goblin scout.",
      },
    }).patch.createDrafts!;

  return {
    campaignId: "campaign-1",
    sessionId: "session-1",
    conversationId: "conversation-1",
    gameSystemId: "dnd5e-2014",
    messageText: "create goblin scout",
    candidateSelectionPolicy: { decision: "resolved" },
    request: null,
    prefilledCreateFieldValues: {
      [ENTITY_FIELD_TYPES.DESCRIPTION]: "A sneaky goblin scout.",
    },
    userId: "user-1",
    campaignMemberId: "member-1",
    role: "OWNER" as unknown as CreateMachineContextState["role"],
    workflowLifecycle: null,
    snapshotRef: null,
    requestEventId: null,
    requestEventSequence: null,
    services: ({
      createEntity: async () => ({ entityId: "entity-1" }),
      writeInitialFieldValues: async () => ({ fieldIds: [] }),
      writeInitialStatBlockValues: async () => ({ statBlockId: null }),
      writeInitialProgressionValues: async () => ({
        progressionId: null,
        progressionLevelIds: [],
      }),
      writeInitialEntityRelations: async () => ({ relationIds: [] }),
      emitPrompt: async () => undefined,
      dispatch: () => undefined,
      dispatchMode: "immediate",
    }) as unknown as CreateMachineContextState["services"],
    dispatch: null,
    dispatchMode: null,
    activeFlowId: "flow-1",
    flowStartedAt: new Date(Date.now()).toISOString(),
    timeoutAt: new Date(Date.now() + 60_000).toISOString(),
    entityId: "entity-1",
    responseSpec: null,
    createPlan,
    createDrafts,
    planningBlockers: [],
    terminalOutcome: null,
    ...overrides,
  };
}

describe("create-machine Patch B helpers", () => {
  it("builds a confirmation-capable final review response spec", () => {
    const context = buildContext({
      createDrafts: {
        ...buildContext().createDrafts!,
        coreDraft: {
          ...buildContext().createDrafts!.coreDraft,
          title: "Goblin Scout",
        },
      },
    });

import type { EntityType } from "../../../../domain/entities/core/types";
import type { CreatePlan } from "../../resolve-create-plan";
import type { EntityCreationDraftBundle } from "../../resolve-entity-creation-drafts";
import type { WorkflowFeedbackItem } from "../workflow-registry";
import type { CreateMachineActorPatch } from "../../contracts/create-machine-actor-patch";

/**
 * Source tags for the explicit root → create adjudication seam.
 *
 * Keep this narrow so root can distinguish dialogue edits from later review
 * reopen flows without guessing from payload shape alone.
 */
export type CreateDraftMutationSource =
  | "response_dialogue"
  | "review_reopen";

export type CreateMachineSignalEvent = {
  type: "CREATE_MACHINE.SIGNAL";
  flowId: string;
  status: CreateMachineWorkflowStatus;
  summary: string;
  patch: CreateMachineActorPatch;
  feedback?: WorkflowFeedbackItem[];
};

export type CreateMachineSaveSucceededEvent = {
  type: "CREATE_MACHINE.SAVE_SUCCEEDED";
  flowId: string;
  entityType: EntityType;
  entityId: string;
  createDrafts: EntityCreationDraftBundle;
};

export type CreateMachineFailedEvent = {
  type: "CREATE_MACHINE.FAILED";
  flowId: string;
  reasons: string[];
  createDrafts: EntityCreationDraftBundle;
};

export type CreateDialogueProposalSubmittedEvent = {
  type: "CREATE_DIALOGUE.PROPOSAL_SUBMITTED";
  payload: {
    flowId: string;
    baseDraftRevision: number;
    proposedDrafts: EntityCreationDraftBundle;
    feedback?: WorkflowFeedbackItem[];
    source?: CreateDraftMutationSource;
  };
};

export type CreateMachineAdjudicateProposalPayload = {
  flowId: string;
  baseDraftRevision: number;
  createPlan: CreatePlan;
  currentDrafts: EntityCreationDraftBundle;
  proposedDrafts: EntityCreationDraftBundle;
  source: CreateDraftMutationSource;
};

export type CreateMachineAdjudicateProposalEvent = {
  type: "CREATE_MACHINE.ADJUDICATE_PROPOSAL";
  payload: CreateMachineAdjudicateProposalPayload;
};

export type CreateMachineConfirmSavePayload = {
  flowId: string;
  draftRevision: number;
};

export type CreateMachineConfirmSaveEvent = {
  type: "CREATE_MACHINE.CONFIRM_SAVE";
  payload: CreateMachineConfirmSavePayload;
};

export type CreateMachineEvent =
  | CreateMachineSignalEvent
  | CreateMachineSaveSucceededEvent
  | CreateMachineFailedEvent
  | CreateMachineAdjudicateProposalEvent
  | CreateMachineConfirmSaveEvent;

export type CreateMachineWorkflowStatus =
  | "need_info"
  | "review_ready"
  | "need_confirmation";

export function resolveActiveWorkflowMatch(input: {
  activeFlows: Record<string, WorkflowRecord>;
  focusedFlowId: string | null;
  recentFlowIds: string[];
}): WorkflowRecord | null {
  const focused = input.focusedFlowId
    ? input.activeFlows[input.focusedFlowId] ?? null
    : null;
  if (focused) return focused;

  for (let index = input.recentFlowIds.length - 1; index >= 0; index -= 1) {
    const flowId = input.recentFlowIds[index];
    const matched = input.activeFlows[flowId];
    if (matched) return matched;
  }

  return null;
}

function isEligibleCreateWorkflowRecord(
  record: WorkflowRecord | null,
): record is WorkflowRecord {
  return (
    !!record &&
    record.module === "create" &&
    record.workflowType === "entity_create" &&
    !TERMINAL_WORKFLOW_STATUSES.has(record.status)
  );
}

function compareWorkflowRecency(
  left: WorkflowRecord,
  right: WorkflowRecord,
): number {
  if (left.timing.updatedAt !== right.timing.updatedAt) {
    return left.timing.updatedAt - right.timing.updatedAt;
  }

  if (left.timing.lastTransitionAt !== right.timing.lastTransitionAt) {
    return left.timing.lastTransitionAt - right.timing.lastTransitionAt;
  }

  status: WorkflowStatus | null | undefined,

export function pushRecentFlowId(
  recentFlowIds: string[],
  flowId: string,
): string[] {
  const withoutFlow = recentFlowIds.filter((id) => id !== flowId);
  return [...withoutFlow, flowId];
}

function normalizeWorkflowFlowId(value: string | null | undefined): string | null {
  if (typeof value !== "string") {
    return null;
  }

  const trimmed = value.trim();
  return trimmed.length > 0 ? trimmed : null;
}

import {
  buildWorkflowRecordFromStartEvent,
  areWorkflowValuesEqual,
  patchWorkflowRecord,
  pushRecentFlowId,
  isStaleCreateDialogueProposal,
  buildStaleCreateDialogueProposalFeedback,
  resolveCreateWorkflowDraftRevision,
  isWellFormedCreateWorkflowStartEventPayload,
  type WorkflowStatus,
  type WorkflowAwaitingReviewEventPayload,
  type WorkflowBlockedEventPayload,
  type WorkflowCompleteEventPayload,
  type CreateDialogueProposalSubmittedPayload,
  type WorkflowFeedbackItem,
  type WorkflowNeedConfirmationEventPayload,
  type WorkflowNeedInfoEventPayload,
  type WorkflowRecord,
  type WorkflowStartEventPayload,
  type WorkflowStartProjection,
  type WorkflowTimedOutEventPayload,
  type WorkflowUpdateEventPayload,
} from "./shared/workflow-registry";
import { evaluateCreateDraftMutationAuthority } from "./shared/create-draft-mutation-policy";
import type {
  ChatSendRootMachineV2Context,
  RootWorkflowAwaitingReviewEvent,
  RootWorkflowBlockedEvent,
  RootWorkflowCompleteEvent,
  RootWorkflowNeedConfirmationEvent,
  RootWorkflowNeedInfoEvent,
  RootWorkflowStartEvent,
  RootWorkflowTimedOutEvent,
  RootWorkflowUpdateEvent,
} from "../chatsendrootmachinev2";

const TERMINAL_WORKFLOW_STATUSES = new Set<WorkflowStatus>([
  "blocked",
  "timed_out",
  "completed",
  "cancelled",
  "errored",
]);

function resolveOptionalIntentFlag(
  intent: "found" | "not_found" | null,
): boolean | undefined {
  if (intent === "found") return true;
  if (intent === "not_found") return false;
  return undefined;
}

function isStaleOrDuplicateWorkflowEvent(
  record: WorkflowRecord,
  payload: {
    lastRequestEventId?: string;
    lastEventSequence?: number;
  },
): boolean {
  if (
    typeof payload.lastRequestEventId === "string" &&
    payload.lastRequestEventId.length > 0 &&
    payload.lastRequestEventId === record.lastRequestEventId
  ) {
    return true;
  }

  if (
    typeof payload.lastEventSequence === "number" &&
    Number.isFinite(payload.lastEventSequence) &&
    typeof record.lastEventSequence === "number" &&
    Number.isFinite(record.lastEventSequence) &&
    payload.lastEventSequence <= record.lastEventSequence
  ) {
    return true;
  }

  return false;
}

function buildTerminalWorkflowCleanup(context: ChatSendRootMachineV2Context, flowId: string) {
  const nextActiveFlows = { ...context.activeFlows };
  delete nextActiveFlows[flowId];

  return {
    activeFlows: nextActiveFlows,
    focusedFlowId:
      context.focusedFlowId === flowId ? null : context.focusedFlowId,
    recentFlowIds: pushRecentFlowId(context.recentFlowIds, flowId),
  };

import { createActor, waitFor } from "xstate";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

import { machine as createDialogueMachine } from "../../src/stately-studio/create-dialogue-machine";
import {
  hydrateRestartableCreateDialogues,
  normalizeWorkflowHydrationState,
} from "../../src/stately-studio/actors/shared/restart-hydration";
import { buildCreateDialogueResumeInput } from "../../src/stately-studio/actors/shared/workflow-registry";
import type {
  WorkflowCreateFlowPhase,
  WorkflowRecord,
  WorkflowResponseBaseline,
} from "../../src/stately-studio/actors/shared/workflow-registry";
import type { AnyResponseSpec } from "../../src/stately-studio/actors/shared/response/response-types";
import type { CreatePlan } from "../../src/stately-studio/actors/resolve-create-plan";
import {
  CANON_STATES,
  ENTITY_TYPES,
} from "../../src/domain/entities/core/types";
import {
  ENTITY_FIELD_TYPES,
  FIELD_VALUE_KINDS,
} from "../../src/domain/entities/fields/types";

function buildCreatePlan(flowId: string): CreatePlan {
  return {
    flowId,
    campaignId: "campaign-1",
    userId: "user-1",
    campaignMemberId: "member-1",
    role: "OWNER" as never,
    gameSystemId: null,
    core: {
      entityType: ENTITY_TYPES.NPC,
      title: "",
      titleLower: "",
      canonState: CANON_STATES.CANON,
    },
    fields: [
      {
        fieldType: ENTITY_FIELD_TYPES.DESCRIPTION,
        valueKind: FIELD_VALUE_KINDS.TEXT,
        orderIndex: 0,
        required: true,
      },
    ],
    statBlocks: [],
    progression: {
      required: false,
      columns: [],
    },
    relations: [],
  };
}

function buildResponseSpec(flowId: string): AnyResponseSpec {
  return {
    promptId: `prompt-${flowId}`,
    promptKind: "workflow",
    requesterMachine: "content_create",
    relatedFlowId: flowId,
    expectedReplyType: null,
    payload: { flowId },
    cards: [],
    options: [],
    citations: [],
  } as unknown as AnyResponseSpec;
}

function buildResponseBaseline(
  flowId: string,
  createDrafts: Record<string, unknown>,
  responseSpec: AnyResponseSpec,
): WorkflowResponseBaseline {
  return {
    flowId,
    draftRevision: 0,
    createDrafts: createDrafts as never,
    responseSpec,
    sentAt: 0,
  };
}

function buildWorkflowRecord(input: {
  flowId: string;
  status?: WorkflowRecord["status"];
  phase?: WorkflowCreateFlowPhase | null;
  currentMachineStateValue?: unknown;
  timingOffsetMs?: number;
  includePendingProposal?: boolean;
}): WorkflowRecord {
  const flowId = input.flowId;
  const createPlan = buildCreatePlan(flowId);
  const createDrafts = { flowId } as Record<string, unknown>;
  const responseSpec = buildResponseSpec(flowId);
  const responseBaseline = buildResponseBaseline(flowId, createDrafts, responseSpec);
  const phase = input.phase ?? "review_ready";
  const timingBase = 1_000 + (input.timingOffsetMs ?? 0);

  return {
    flowId,
    module: "create",
    workflowType: "entity_create",
    status: input.status ?? "awaiting_review",
    responseSpec,
    createPlan,
    createDrafts,
    responseBaseline,
    pendingCreateDialogueProposals: input.includePendingProposal
      ? [
          {
            flowId,
            baseDraftRevision: 0,
            proposedDrafts: createDrafts as never,
          },
        ]
      : undefined,
    activePrompt: null,
    lastResponseId: null,
    feedback: [],
    currentMachineStateValue: input.currentMachineStateValue ?? phase,
    snapshotRef: {
      phase: input.phase ?? undefined,
      draftRevision: 0,
      responseBaselineRevision: 0,
    },
    timing: {
      createdAt: timingBase,
      updatedAt: timingBase + 10,
      lastTransitionAt: timingBase + 20,
      waitingForUserSince: null,
      blockedAt: null,
      timedOutAt: null,
      completedAt: null,
      cancelledAt: null,
      erroredAt: null,
      expiresAt: null,
    },
  } as WorkflowRecord;
}

describe("restart hydration", () => {
  it("falls back to currentMachineStateValue when snapshotRef.phase is absent", () => {
    const record = buildWorkflowRecord({
      flowId: "flow-1",
      phase: null,
      currentMachineStateValue: "save_pending",
    });

    const resumeInput = buildCreateDialogueResumeInput({
      record,
      campaignEntityIndex: null,
    });

    expect(resumeInput?.phase).toBe("save_pending");
    expect(resumeInput?.flowId).toBe("flow-1");
  });

  it("rejects blank flow IDs during create-dialogue hydration", async () => {
    const actor = createActor(createDialogueMachine, {
      input: {
        flowId: "   ",
        phase: "review_ready",
        draftRevision: 0,
        createPlan: null,
        createDrafts: null,
        responseSpec: null,
        responseBaseline: null,
        feedback: [],
        campaignEntityIndex: null,
      },
    });

    actor.start();

    const snapshot = await waitFor(actor, (state) => state.status === "done", {
      timeout: 2_500,
    });

    expect(snapshot.output?.status).toBe("failed");
    expect(snapshot.output?.error).toBe("create_dialogue_hydration_failed");

    actor.stop();
  });

  it("merges the supervised child patch into root hydration state", async () => {
    const record = buildWorkflowRecord({
      flowId: "flow-1",
      phase: "review_ready",
      currentMachineStateValue: "review_ready",
      status: "awaiting_review",
    });

    const normalized = normalizeWorkflowHydrationState({
      activeFlows: {
        [record.flowId]: record,
      },
      focusedFlowId: record.flowId,
      recentFlowIds: [record.flowId],
    });

    const hydrated = await hydrateRestartableCreateDialogues({
      context: {
        ...normalized,
        campaignEntityIndex: null,
      },
    });

    expect(hydrated.patch.focusedFlowId).toBe("flow-1");
    expect(hydrated.patch.currentTurn?.currentMachineStateValue).toBe("review_ready");
    expect(hydrated.patch.hydratedCreateDialogueFlowIds).toEqual(["flow-1"]);
    expect(hydrated.patch.hydratedCreateDialoguePhasesByFlowId?.["flow-1"]).toBe(
      "review_ready",
    );
  });

  it("canonicalizes whitespace-padded responseSpec related flow IDs during restart hydration", () => {
    const record = buildWorkflowRecord({
      flowId: "flow-1 ",
      phase: "review_ready",
      currentMachineStateValue: "review_ready",
    });

    const normalized = normalizeWorkflowHydrationState({
      activeFlows: {
        [record.flowId]: record,
      },
      focusedFlowId: record.flowId,
      recentFlowIds: [record.flowId],
    });

    expect(Object.keys(normalized.activeFlows)).toEqual(["flow-1"]);
    expect(normalized.activeFlows["flow-1"].responseSpec?.relatedFlowId).toBe("flow-1");
    expect(
      normalized.activeFlows["flow-1"].responseBaseline?.responseSpec?.relatedFlowId,
    ).toBe("flow-1");
  });

  it("rewrites persisted responseSpec flow IDs for renamed hydrated flows", () => {
    const firstRecord = buildWorkflowRecord({
      flowId: "flow-duplicate",
      phase: "review_ready",
      currentMachineStateValue: "review_ready",
      timingOffsetMs: 0,
    });
    const secondRecord = buildWorkflowRecord({
      flowId: "flow-duplicate",
      phase: "save_pending",
      currentMachineStateValue: "save_pending",
      timingOffsetMs: 100,
    });

    const normalized = normalizeWorkflowHydrationState({
      activeFlows: {
        first: firstRecord,
        second: secondRecord,
      },
      focusedFlowId: "flow-duplicate",
      recentFlowIds: ["flow-duplicate", "flow-duplicate__hydrated_2"],
    });

    expect(
      normalized.activeFlows["flow-duplicate__hydrated_2"].responseSpec?.relatedFlowId,
    ).toBe("flow-duplicate__hydrated_2");
    expect(
      normalized.activeFlows["flow-duplicate__hydrated_2"].responseBaseline?.responseSpec
        ?.relatedFlowId,
    ).toBe("flow-duplicate__hydrated_2");
  });

  it("skips malformed pending create-dialogue proposals during restart normalization", () => {
    const record = buildWorkflowRecord({
      flowId: "flow-1",
      phase: "review_ready",
      currentMachineStateValue: "review_ready",
    });

    const normalized = normalizeWorkflowHydrationState({
      activeFlows: {
        [record.flowId]: {
          ...record,
          pendingCreateDialogueProposals: [
            null as never,
            undefined as never,
            {
              flowId: record.flowId,
              baseDraftRevision: 0,
            } as never,
            {
              flowId: record.flowId,
              baseDraftRevision: 0,
              proposedDrafts: record.createDrafts as never,
            },
          ],
        },
      },
      focusedFlowId: record.flowId,
      recentFlowIds: [record.flowId],
    });

    expect(
      normalized.activeFlows[record.flowId].pendingCreateDialogueProposals,
    ).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(
      normalized.activeFlows[record.flowId].pendingCreateDialogueProposals?.[0].flowId,
    ).toBe(record.flowId);
  });

  it("keeps duplicate source flow IDs collision-safe and rewrites nested flow-bearing fields", async () => {
    const firstRecord = buildWorkflowRecord({
      flowId: "flow-duplicate",
      phase: "review_ready",
      currentMachineStateValue: "review_ready",
      timingOffsetMs: 0,
      includePendingProposal: true,
    });
    const secondRecord = buildWorkflowRecord({
      flowId: "flow-duplicate",
      phase: "save_pending",
      currentMachineStateValue: "save_pending",
      timingOffsetMs: 100,
      includePendingProposal: true,
    });

    const normalized = normalizeWorkflowHydrationState({
      activeFlows: {
        first: firstRecord,
        second: secondRecord,
      },
      focusedFlowId: "flow-duplicate",
      recentFlowIds: ["flow-duplicate", "flow-duplicate__hydrated_2"],
    });

    const hydrated = await hydrateRestartableCreateDialogues({
      context: {
        ...normalized,
        campaignEntityIndex: null,
      },
    });

    expect(Object.keys(normalized.activeFlows).sort()).toEqual([
      "flow-duplicate",
      "flow-duplicate__hydrated_2",
    ]);
    expect(normalized.focusedFlowId).toBe("flow-duplicate");
    expect(normalized.recentFlowIds).toEqual([
      "flow-duplicate",
      "flow-duplicate__hydrated_2",
    ]);

    expect(normalized.activeFlows["flow-duplicate"].createPlan?.flowId).toBe(
      "flow-duplicate",
    );
    expect(
      normalized.activeFlows["flow-duplicate__hydrated_2"].createPlan?.flowId,
    ).toBe("flow-duplicate__hydrated_2");
    expect(
      normalized.activeFlows["flow-duplicate__hydrated_2"].pendingCreateDialogueProposals?.[0]
        .flowId,
    ).toBe("flow-duplicate__hydrated_2");

    expect(hydrated.hydratedFlowIds).toEqual([
      "flow-duplicate",
      "flow-duplicate__hydrated_2",
    ]);
    expect(hydrated.patch.focusedFlowId).toBe("flow-duplicate");
    expect(hydrated.patch.currentTurn?.currentMachineStateValue).toBe("review_ready");
  });
});

import type { CreatePlan } from "../resolve-create-plan";
import type {
  EntityCreationDraftBundle,
  EntityFieldDraftEntry,
  EntityRelationDraftEntry,
  EntityStatBlockDraft,
  EntityStatBlockDraftEntry,
} from "../resolve-entity-creation-drafts";
import type { WorkflowFeedbackItem } from "./workflow-registry";
import { areWorkflowValuesEqual } from "./workflow-registry";
import type { CreateDraftMutationSource } from "./protocols/create-engine-events";

export type CreateDraftMutationAuthorityInput = {
  flowId: string;
  createPlan: CreatePlan;
  currentDrafts: EntityCreationDraftBundle;
  proposedDrafts: EntityCreationDraftBundle;
  source?: CreateDraftMutationSource;
};

export type CreateDraftMutationAuthorityResult = {
  allowed: boolean;
  feedback: WorkflowFeedbackItem[];
};

type CreateDraftMutationViolationCode =
  | "wrong_flow_id"
  | "create_plan_mismatch"
  | "approved_not_allowed"
  | "addressed_not_allowed"
  | "draft_surface_mismatch";

function buildFeedback(
  target: string,
  message: string,
  code: CreateDraftMutationViolationCode,
): WorkflowFeedbackItem {
  return { target, message, code };
}

function resolveSource(
  source: CreateDraftMutationSource | undefined,
): CreateDraftMutationSource {
  return source ?? "response_dialogue";
}

function hasIllegalApprovalTransition(input: {
  currentStatus: string | null | undefined;
  proposedStatus: string | null | undefined;
}): boolean {
  return input.currentStatus !== "approved" && input.proposedStatus === "approved";
}

function hasIllegalAddressedTransition(input: {
  currentAddressed: boolean | null | undefined;
  proposedAddressed: boolean | null | undefined;
}): boolean {
  return input.currentAddressed !== true && input.proposedAddressed === true;
}

function collectDuplicateKeys<T>(
  entries: readonly T[],
  resolveKey: (entry: T) => string,
): string[] {
  const seen = new Set<string>();
  const duplicates = new Set<string>();

  for (const entry of entries) {
    const key = resolveKey(entry);

    if (seen.has(key)) {
      duplicates.add(key);
      continue;
    }

    seen.add(key);
  }

  return [...duplicates];
}

function compareCoreDraft(input: {
  createPlan: CreatePlan;
  currentDraft: EntityCreationDraftBundle["coreDraft"];
  proposedDraft: EntityCreationDraftBundle["coreDraft"];
  feedback: WorkflowFeedbackItem[];
}): boolean {
  let allowed = true;

  if (
    input.proposedDraft.entityType !== input.createPlan.core.entityType ||
    input.proposedDraft.canonState !== input.createPlan.core.canonState
  ) {
    input.feedback.push(
      buildFeedback(
        "core",
        "core draft no longer matches the active create plan.",
        "create_plan_mismatch",
      ),
    );
    allowed = false;
  }

  if (
    hasIllegalApprovalTransition({
      currentStatus: input.currentDraft.status,
      proposedStatus: input.proposedDraft.status,
    })
  ) {
    input.feedback.push(
      buildFeedback(
        "core",
        "core identity cannot be marked approved by the dialogue child.",
        "approved_not_allowed",
      ),
    );
    allowed = false;
  }

  return allowed;
}

function compareFieldDrafts(input: {
  createPlan: CreatePlan;
  currentDrafts: EntityCreationDraftBundle["fieldDrafts"];
  proposedDrafts: EntityCreationDraftBundle["fieldDrafts"];
  feedback: WorkflowFeedbackItem[];
}): boolean {
  let allowed = true;

  const duplicateFieldTypes = collectDuplicateKeys(
    input.proposedDrafts.entries,
    (entry) => entry.fieldType,
  );

  for (const fieldType of duplicateFieldTypes) {
    input.feedback.push(
      buildFeedback(
        `field:${fieldType}`,
        `${fieldType} appears more than once in the proposed draft bundle.`,
        "draft_surface_mismatch",
      ),
    );
    allowed = false;
  }

  if (
    hasIllegalApprovalTransition({
      currentStatus: input.currentDrafts.status,
      proposedStatus: input.proposedDrafts.status,
    })
  ) {
    input.feedback.push(
      buildFeedback(
        "fieldDrafts",
        "field draft collection cannot be marked approved by the dialogue child.",
        "approved_not_allowed",
      ),
    );
    allowed = false;
  }

  const currentByFieldType = new Map(
    input.currentDrafts.entries.map((entry) => [entry.fieldType, entry] as const),
  );
  const proposedByFieldType = new Map(
    input.proposedDrafts.entries.map((entry) => [entry.fieldType, entry] as const),
  );
  const planFieldTypes = new Set(input.createPlan.fields.map((field) => field.fieldType));

  for (const planField of input.createPlan.fields) {
    const proposed = proposedByFieldType.get(planField.fieldType) ?? null;
    const current = currentByFieldType.get(planField.fieldType) ?? null;

    if (!proposed) {
      input.feedback.push(
        buildFeedback(
          `field:${planField.fieldType}`,
          `${planField.fieldType} is missing from the proposed draft bundle.`,
          "draft_surface_mismatch",

  validFromTick: bigint | null;
  validToTick: bigint | null;
  metadata: Record<string, unknown> | null;
};

export type EntityRelationDraftCollection = DraftArtifactState & {
  entries: EntityRelationDraftEntry[];
};

export type EntityCreationDraftBundle = {
  coreDraft: EntityCoreDraft;
  fieldDrafts: EntityFieldDraftCollection;
  statBlockDrafts: EntityStatBlockDraft[];
  progressionDraft: EntityProgressionDraft;
  relationDrafts: EntityRelationDraftCollection;
};

type ResolveEntityCreationDraftsPatch = {
  createDrafts: EntityCreationDraftBundle | null;
  planningBlockers: string[];
  terminalOutcome?: "blocked";
};

export type ResolveEntityCreationDraftsOutput =
  ActorPatchedTerminal<ResolveEntityCreationDraftsPatch>;

function sortByOrderIndex<T extends { orderIndex: number }>(entries: readonly T[]): T[] {
  return [...entries].sort((left, right) => left.orderIndex - right.orderIndex);
}

function buildInitialDraftArtifactState(): DraftArtifactState {
  return { status: "incomplete" };
}

function buildInitialDraftEntryState(): DraftEntryState {
  return {
    addressed: false,
    status: "incomplete",
  };
}

function buildCoreDraft(createPlan: CreatePlan): EntityCoreDraft {
  return {
    ...buildInitialDraftArtifactState(),
    entityType: createPlan.core.entityType,
    title: createPlan.core.title,
    titleLower: createPlan.core.titleLower,
    canonState: createPlan.core.canonState,
  };
}

type EnsureCampaignEntityIndexInput = {
  campaignId: string;
  currentIndex: CampaignEntityIndex | null;
};

async function ensureCampaignEntityIndex(
  input: EnsureCampaignEntityIndexInput,
): Promise<CampaignEntityIndex | null> {
  const campaignId = input.campaignId.trim();
  if (!campaignId) return input.currentIndex ?? null;

  if (input.currentIndex && input.currentIndex.campaignId === campaignId) {
    await refreshCampaignEntityIndex({
      db: prisma,
      index: input.currentIndex,
    });
    return input.currentIndex;
  }

  return buildCampaignEntityIndex({
    db: prisma,
    campaignId,
  });
}

function hasDurableRootIdentity(
  context: ChatSendRootMachineV2Context,
): boolean {
  return (
    context.campaignId.length > 0 &&
    context.sessionId.length > 0 &&
    context.conversationId.length > 0
  );
}

function resolveAuthorizedRequestIdentity(
  context: ChatSendRootMachineV2Context,
): {
  userId: string;
  campaignMemberId: string;
  role: CampaignRole;
} | null {
  const request = context.currentTurn.request;
  if (!request || typeof request !== "object") return null;
  const record = request as Record<string, unknown>;

  const userId =
    typeof record.userId === "string" ? record.userId.trim() : "";
  const campaignMemberId =
    typeof record.campaignMemberId === "string"
      ? record.campaignMemberId.trim()
      : "";
  const roleValue = typeof record.role === "string" ? record.role.trim() : "";
  const role = Object.values(CampaignRole).includes(roleValue as CampaignRole)
    ? (roleValue as CampaignRole)
    : null;

  if (!userId || !campaignMemberId || !role) return null;
  return { userId, campaignMemberId, role };
}

function hasAuthorizedRequestIdentity(
  context: ChatSendRootMachineV2Context,
): boolean {
  return resolveAuthorizedRequestIdentity(context) !== null;
}

function resolveCreateDialogueLookupRequestEntityFromIndex(input: {
  campaignEntityIndex: CampaignEntityIndex | null;
  request: CreateDialogueLookupRequestPayload;
}): { entityId: string | null; entityLabel: string | null } {
  const entityId = asNonEmptyString(input.request.lookup.entityId);
  const entityLabel = asNonEmptyString(input.request.lookup.entityLabel);

  if (!input.campaignEntityIndex) {
    return { entityId, entityLabel };
  }

  if (entityId) {
    const byId = input.campaignEntityIndex.byId.get(entityId) ?? null;
    if (byId) {
      return { entityId: byId.id, entityLabel: byId.title };
    }
  }

  if (entityLabel) {
    const exactMatches = findByTitle(input.campaignEntityIndex, entityLabel);
    if (exactMatches.length === 1) {
      return { entityId: exactMatches[0].id, entityLabel: exactMatches[0].title };
    }

{"end_line":190,"file":"src/stately-studio/actors/shared/restart-hydration.ts","kind":"fn","source":"function normalizePendingCreateDialogueProposals(\n  proposals: WorkflowRecord[\"pendingCreateDialogueProposals\"],\n  canonicalFlowId: string,\n): WorkflowRecord[\"pendingCreateDialogueProposals\"] {\n  if (!Array.isArray(proposals)) {\n    return proposals;\n  }\n\n  let changed = false;\n  const normalizedProposals = proposals.reduce<CreateDialogueProposalSubmittedPayload[]>(\n    (accumulator, proposal) => {\n      if (!isValidPendingCreateDialogueProposal(proposal)) {\n        changed = true;\n        return accumulator;\n      }\n\n      const proposalFlowId = normalizeWorkflowFlowId(proposal.flowId);\n      if (proposalFlowId !== canonicalFlowId) {\n        changed = true;\n        accumulator.push({\n          ...proposal,\n          flowId: canonicalFlowId,\n        });\n        return accumulator;\n      }\n\n      accumulator.push(proposal as CreateDialogueProposalSubmittedPayload);\n      return accumulator;\n    },\n    [],\n  );\n\n  return changed || normalizedProposals.length !== proposals.length\n    ? normalizedProposals\n    : proposals;\n}","start_line":155,"symbol":"normalizePendingCreateDialogueProposals"}
